{"parentUuid":"d9332af4-7452-4821-9057-e4c4ca4a614b","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a51977fc151ec54f9","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdqWjGz5yXRw2VG6y4Ev4","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WfWhaJnAqGZNDYhouDHE9F","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/Users/vladmihaimiron/Documents/DebateAI-V3/docs/missions/2026-08-06-v3-programming/ratification/citation-routes.md","offset":563,"limit":362},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":29108,"cache_read_input_tokens":30556,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":29108,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdqWjFjvwxszyF2duiArw","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"45b0d12e-8fa7-4605-8e07-ca1c2be77f8d","timestamp":"2026-08-08T15:24:58.003Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/vladmihaimiron/Documents/DebateAI-V3","sessionId":"a8fcf2e3-bdd1-4a10-bcd6-3ec887012907","version":"2.1.221","gitBranch":"main","slug":"hidden-riding-bengio"}
{"parentUuid":"45b0d12e-8fa7-4605-8e07-ca1c2be77f8d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"78381ab9-ed25-46c6-b47e-ef1a461a0fe0","agentId":"a51977fc151ec54f9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WfWhaJnAqGZNDYhouDHE9F","type":"tool_result","content":"563\t\n564\t**Claim.** No citation failure satisfies two routes.\n565\t\n566\t**Proof.** Two mechanisms, both required.\n567\t\n568\t1. **Within a rung**, the branches are pairwise exclusive: R1/R2 because **§3.1's\n569\t   truth table assigns each predicate combination exactly one outcome**; R3/R4\n570\t   because **E-7 admits exactly one access-depth value**, and §3.2's derivation\n571\t   yields exactly one value **per attempt**; rungs 3–6 are booleans.\n572\t2. **Across rungs**, the rung order is **total and fixed**, and the route is the\n573\t   **first** rung that fails. Every later rung has an **earlier rung's pass as its\n574\t   precondition** — R5 through R8 are unreachable unless the source is\n575\t   `OPENED_FULL` (rung 2 passed), R6 through R8 unless the artifact is current\n576\t   (rung 3 passed), R7 and R8 unless a comparison result exists (rung 4 passed),\n577\t   R8 unless the span is present (rung 5 passed). So a second failing condition\n578\t   **cannot re-route an attempt that already has a route**.\n579\t\n580\tBecause the order is load-bearing, it is a **ratified property of the enum, not an\n581\timplementation choice** — the same status `02-data-model.md` gives\n582\t`snapshot_at_seq`'s ordering (*\"the ordering is the point\"*). ∎\n583\t\n584\t### 5.3 Reachability — no dead member\n585\t\n586\t**Claim.** Each of the eight has at least one producer that can actually fire.\n587\t\n588\t**Why it is owed.** `02-data-model.md` §13 applies AC-77 / charter VR-4 to\n589\t`tier_source`'s `DERIVED`: *\"a member with no producer must be removed rather than\n590\tleft unreachable.\"* **AC-78** says the same of gates. An enum member that cannot\n591\tfire is shipped-dark vocabulary.\n592\t\n593\t**Producers, one per member** *(rev 2: R3's and R6's restated against the repaired\n594\trungs)*: **R1** — Q17's zero-result projection (E-9). **R2** — Q51's provenance join\n595\tfinding no locator; the AC-92 honesty violation; a named source with no\n596\t`source_record`. **R3** — the attempt recording\n597\t`ACCESS_BLOCKED` at harvest, **or** its access / recheck action returning a non-`OK`\n598\ttyped outcome (§3.2). **R4** — Q16 recording `PREVIEW_ONLY`, **or a Q40 reopen that\n599\tsucceeds at preview depth** (rev 3). **R5** — Q40's reopen observing a different\n600\tversion; E-13's age computation against `as_of`. **R6** — **all five** recorded\n601\treasons have producers: a source-level citation (`NO_SPAN_CITED`), Q16 finding the\n602\tmedium uncomparable (`MEDIUM_UNSUPPORTED`), a comparison owed with no entry\n603\t(`COMPARE_NOT_EXECUTED`), a comparison entry with any non-`OK` outcome\n604\t(`COMPARE_EXECUTION_NOT_OK`), and an `OK` entry whose result did not persist\n605\t(`COMPARE_RESULT_MISSING`). **R7** — Q40's `not-found`. **R8** — Q40's `deviates`.\n606\t\n607\t**Fixture obligation.** One **firing demonstration per route**, riding on\n608\t**`FX-HR-H5`** at **S6** — spec **§22**'s acceptance table states the discipline:\n609\t*\"a blocking gate with no firing demonstration is an untested claim\"*\n610\t(`CARRIED-DESIGN`, Grok F5). **Fixture ids are not minted here**; assigning them\n611\tbelongs to `06-test-strategy.md`.\n612\t\n613\t### 5.4 No disguised \"other\" — the R2 test\n614\t\n615\t**DR-084 forbids a generic \"other\".** R2 is the member a reviewer must test, because\n616\tit occupies the position an \"other\" would occupy. Three properties distinguish them:\n617\t\n618\t| | a generic `OTHER` | **R2 `CITATION_UNBACKED`** |\n619\t|---|---|---|\n620\t| **Definition** | negative — *\"none of the other seven\"* | **positive** — *the evidence store does not carry the retrieval this citation names* |\n621\t| **Decidability** | only **after** the other seven have been evaluated and failed | **before any of them** — it is their shared **precondition failure** |\n622\t| **Consequence** | none of its own; it inherits a default | **its own**: Q51's serve block and the reasoning-only downgrade |\n623\t\n624\tR2 is evaluated **first**, and the six routes below rung 1 are **unreachable** when\n625\tit fires, because every one of them requires the complete retrieval record (`P_rec`)\n626\tthat R2 says is absent. **R1 shares rung 1 with it and is separated by a single\n627\tpredicate** — `P_abs`, whether the store carries the honest negative — not by\n628\telimination. An \"other\" is a bucket at the end of a list; **R2 is the root of the\n629\tladder**. Adding a hypothetical ninth failure mode would not enlarge R2: it would\n630\thave to attach to a rung, and **every rung is total** (§5.1). ∎\n631\t\n632\t### 5.5 The boundary — five cases that are *not* citation failures, with their homes\n633\t\n634\tResidue can also hide **outside** the enum, by quietly excluding cases. Each\n635\texclusion is named and re-homed:\n636\t\n637\t| Case | Why it is out of domain | Its typed home |\n638\t|---|---|---|\n639\t| The unknown was **never searched for** | no citation attempt was made | abstention kind **1** (*not searched*), §12.3 Home 1 |\n640\t| A claim resting on **reasoning alone** | no source was bound | **Q51's reasoning-only downgrade** + AC-24's way-of-knowing band ceiling |\n641\t| Evidence **off-subject** or **partly relevant** | admissibility, not citation | **E-5**'s reject / admit-downgraded; `OFF-SUBJECT-DOWNGRADE` (§12.3 #20) |\n642\t| The **same source counted twice** | provenance clustering, not citation | **E-10**'s cluster gate; `evidence_item`'s cluster key |\n643\t| A **served number** that fails replay | replay, not citation | **S-9e**'s eviction; `MISSING-NUMBER` (§12.3 #22); `served_number_event.status = EVICTED` |\n644\t\n645\t**And one case that could have become residue and does not:** an attempt whose\n646\tcomparison was **skipped for cost**. It cannot arise — **citation routes are\n647\tprotected core and are never budget-skippable** (**DR-021 knob 9**, extended by\n648\t**DR-052**; spec §4's budget-override row and §21.2). `SKIPPED-BY-BUDGET` is\n649\ttherefore **not** a reachable cause of any route, and any implementation that\n650\tproduces one is a **protected-core violation**, caught by `FX-C52-06`.\n651\t\n652\t---\n653\t\n654\t## 6. The carrier — what `citation_route_record` must hold\n655\t\n656\t`02-data-model.md` §11A.1 declares the table and *\"still mints no member\"*. The\n657\t**membership** is §4's. The **fields the membership needs** are below —\n658\t**a proposal for a fold-in ticket**, not an edit made here.\n659\t\n660\t**Two disciplines constrain the list.** **AC-85** — one authoritative home per\n661\tfact; anything already on `source_record`, `evidence_item` or `ledger_entry` is\n662\t**referenced, never copied**. **AC-88 / `02-data-model.md` §14** — *status is\n663\tderived, never asserted*; nothing derivable is stored.\n664\t\n665\t| Field | Holds | Why it cannot live elsewhere |\n666\t|---|---|---|\n667\t| `run_id`, `node_id` | graph scope | every graph FK is graph-scoped (C-11) |\n668\t| `assertion_ref` | the served or load-bearing claim being cited | Q51 joins provenance **per claim** |\n669\t| `row_id` | the battery row that made the attempt — **`Q16` / `Q40` / `Q51`** | reuses the existing row vocabulary; **mints no new enum** |\n670\t| `at_seq` | position in the run's total order | S-21 — ledger order is total and deterministic |\n671\t| `outcome` | **`VERIFIED` / `ROUTED`** | D2's success is not an enum member (§2) |\n672\t| `route` | the eight-member enum; **non-null iff `outcome = ROUTED`** (`CHECK`) | the ratified membership |\n673\t| `source_ref` | → `evidence.source_record`; null on R1 and R2 | the source's own facts (access depth, locator, archived version) are read **through** this ref |\n674\t| `evidence_item_ref` | → the span; null where no span was cited | AC-85 |\n675\t| `absence_row_ref` | → `evidence.absence_row`; **non-null iff `route = NO_SOURCE_FOUND`** (`CHECK`) | E-9's servable finding |\n676\t| **`ledger_entry_ref`** | → **this attempt's** obliged action rows and their typed outcomes: the **access-depth / locator-resolution / recheck** entry (rung 2) and the **exact-quote-comparison** entry (rung 4) | **A-2** names all of these action kinds · AC-44 · DR-027. **Load-bearing, not incidental**: it settles rung 2's non-`OK` case and R6's reasons ③④⑤ without restating the ledger enum. **Rev 3 correction**: it does **not** settle rung 2's `OK` case — `02-data-model.md` §6.1 declares no typed action-result payload, so an `OK` outcome carries no depth |\n677\t| **`attempt_access_depth`** *(rev 3; scoped in rev 4)* | `OPENED_FULL` / `PREVIEW_ONLY` / `ACCESS_BLOCKED` — **E-7's own three values, no new vocabulary** (§13 inventories them). Rung 2's tested field. **`NULL` exactly when the attempt performed no opening** — i.e. exactly on the rung-1 terminals **R1** and **R2**; **required on every row that descends to rung 2**, so R3…R8 always carry it | **E-7** obliges the depth as a *required record* of an **opening**; §11A.1 places it on `source_record`, which is **per source**. **Four `CHECK`s** (§3.2): null-ness matches the rung-1 terminals · a depth may exist only where an **opening-kind action** does (`ledger_entry.action_kind`, §6.1) · equality with `source_record` on a harvest attempt · `ACCESS_BLOCKED` on a non-`OK` opening outcome. The **reopen-at-a-changed-depth** write is the carrier addition **CARRIER-1** (§9.1) |\n678\t| `opening_action_ref` *(rev 4)* | the limb of `ledger_entry_ref` pointing at this attempt's **opening-kind action**, or null where the attempt opened nothing | Makes `CHECK` 2 expressible. **Action kind is already a declared `ledger_entry` column** (§6.1), so this references a ruled field and adds no vocabulary |\n679\t| `claimed_source_text` | the unresolvable reference, verbatim; **R2 only** | the defect's only evidence — it exists nowhere else by definition |\n680\t| `preview_limb` | `COMPLIANT` / `PROHIBITED_EXTRACTION`; **R4 only** | E-7's prohibition needs its breach recorded, not merely refused |\n681\t| `compare_unavailable_reason` | **`NO_SPAN_CITED` / `MEDIUM_UNSUPPORTED` / `COMPARE_NOT_EXECUTED` / `COMPARE_EXECUTION_NOT_OK` / `COMPARE_RESULT_MISSING`** *(five — rev 3)*; **R6 only**; total as the leaf set of the four ordered booleans at §4 · R6 | E-8's *\"wherever the source supports it\"* is otherwise unfalsifiable. The **non-`OK` outcome itself is never copied** — it is read through `ledger_entry_ref` (AC-85). **Reasons are fields, not routes: five reasons, still eight routes** |\n682\t| `observed_version`, `observed_at` | what this attempt saw; **R5's evidence** | the **archived** version stays on `evidence_item` — this is the *other* side of the comparison |\n683\t| `mismatch_locus` | first differing offset + the two bounded spans; **R8 only** | **no similarity scalar** (DR-039; A-3a's precedent) |\n684\t| `engine_version` | replay identity | S-17 / S-20 |\n685\t\n686\t**Deliberately absent — and every absence is a derivation, not an omission.**\n687\t**No copy of the *harvest* access depth**: `source_record`'s value is read through\n688\t`source_ref`, and `attempt_access_depth` is **`CHECK`-equal to it on the harvest\n689\tattempt** — one fact, one writer, two readers, so AC-85 is not engaged. *(Rev 3\n690\tcorrects rev 2 here: rev 2 claimed the attempt value was **derived** and stored\n691\tnothing, which §6.1's column list does not support. A reopen's depth is a\n692\t**different fact about a different opening**, and recording it is E-7's own\n693\trequirement — see §3.2 and CARRIER-1.)* **No `current_route`**: which\n694\tattempt's route serves is derived from the attempt in force at serve time (§5.1).\n695\t**No `blocks_serve` flag** — derived from `route` and the claim's load-bearing\n696\tstatus. **No `severity` and no score of any kind** — loudness is a **property of\n697\tthe route**, not a number. And **no `is_hard_killed` column** — the gate is not\n698\twritten (§8), and its carrier would be the gate shipped dark.\n699\t\n700\t**Immutability.** One row per attempt, append-only, in `evidence` on the same\n701\tmigration lineage (§11A.1) — the discipline **P10** already applies to receipts.\n702\t\n703\t---\n704\t\n705\t## 7. The spec §12.3 amendment — drafted for V, applied by V (S-13)\n706\t\n707\t**Two of the eight surface to a reader as a typed state** and therefore need a\n708\t§12.3 placement under **DR-084** and **S-13**. Both are **condition marks**\n709\t(Home 2) — *\"servable in parallel\"*, an answer may wear several — because neither\n710\tis an ignorance-ledger unknown (Home 1) and neither ends a run (Home 3).\n711\t\n712\t**The eight routes themselves are NOT placed in §12.3.** They are an internal\n713\t**evidence-subsystem** enum in `packages/kernel` (`02-data-model.md` §13's own\n714\twords: *\"as a closed evidence-subsystem enum (not a condition mark)\"*). Only the\n715\ttwo reader-facing marks below are §12.3's business.\n716\t\n717\t### 7.1 · Draft row — `UNVERIFIED-CITATION`\n718\t\n719\t```\n720\t| 23 | `UNVERIFIED-CITATION` | Q16 · Q40 · §7.3 | a cited source was opened, but nothing in it was compared character-for-character to what the answer says it says |\n721\t```\n722\t\n723\t**Raised by:** route **R6 `EXACT_COMPARE_UNAVAILABLE`**. **Why a reader needs it:**\n724\twithout it a shown quote reads as a checked quote, and E-8's *\"wherever the source\n725\tsupports it\"* becomes a silent pass — Q36's anti-theater law at citation scale.\n726\t**Scope:** `node` or `answer` (`condition_mark.scope`, `02-data-model.md` §13).\n727\t\n728\t### 7.2 · Draft row — `CITATION-RECHECK-FAILED` *(rev 2: renamed and rewritten)*\n729\t\n730\t```\n731\t| 24 | `CITATION-RECHECK-FAILED` | Q40 · §7.3 | a cited span was re-checked against its source and failed — absent, or deviating character-for-character; the span supports nothing |\n732\t```\n733\t\n734\t\n735\t**Raised by:** routes **R7 `SPAN_NOT_FOUND`** and **R8 `SPAN_MISMATCH`** (one mark,\n736\ttwo routes: marks are coarser than routes, exactly as `MISSING-NUMBER` covers any\n737\treplay failure). **Which of the two fired is on the record**, so the mark never has\n738\tto carry the accusation.\n739\t\n740\t**Why it was renamed.** Rev 1 drafted `CITATION-WITHDRAWN`, whose *Says* text —\n741\t*\"was withdrawn; the rest of the answer stands\"* — **asserted a remedy the pack does\n742\tnot rule** (Codex finding 4). S-9e rules eviction of an unreplayable component\n743\t**number**; Q40 rules a typed **outcome**. The rev-2 text states **only** what is\n744\truled: **Q40's failed re-check** and **AC-90's honest zero-information value**\n745\t(*\"proves nothing … never guessed\"*). A mark whose own definition contains an\n746\tunratified behaviour would smuggle that behaviour into §12.3 under S-13 — the exact\n747\tfailure mode this ticket exists to avoid.\n748\t\n749\t**If V wants the withdrawal behaviour**, it is rider **R7/R8-a** (§9.1); the mark's\n750\ttext needs no change to accommodate it, because a withdrawn span is still a span\n751\tthat failed re-check.\n752\t\n753\t**Scope:** `node` or `answer` (`condition_mark.scope`, `02-data-model.md` §13).\n754\t\n755\t### 7.3 · What is deliberately **not** minted, and why\n756\t\n757\t| Route | Why no new state | The existing home |\n758\t|---|---|---|\n759\t| R1 `NO_SOURCE_FOUND` | absence already has a servable form and an abstention kind | `absence_row` (E-9) + Home 1 kind **2** |\n760\t| R2 `CITATION_UNBACKED` | the consequence already has typed states | Q51 downgrade; the `COMPONENTS_ONLY` terminal's **`DEFECT`** badge (S-7); AC-91's prose *why* + *what would unlock it*. **See SP-10** |\n761\t| R3 `SOURCE_UNREACHABLE` | access depth is **already a required served record**, not an adjective | E-7's three-valued record; `blocked_not_lazy` |\n762\t| R4 `PREVIEW_DEPTH_ONLY` | same — the depth **is** the disclosure | E-7's three-valued record |\n763\t| R5 `SOURCE_SUPERSEDED` | **freshness limb**: E-13 already prescribes the surface — refuse, or serve with an **explicit staleness statement**. **Drift limb (rev 2)**: the surface is **the two versions on the citation**, which E-8 already obliges to be archived — **no mark is proposed, because no consequence beyond disclosure is ruled** | E-13's statement; E-8's archived version; `STALE`/`UNDER-REVIEW` (#10/#11) remain **answer-scoped, raised by DR-015's watched trigger — not extended here**. Rider **R5-a** if V wants more |\n764\t\n765\t**Minimum minting is the point.** S-13 makes every mint a founding-spec edit; five\n766\tof the eight need none.\n767\t\n768\t### 7.4 · Consequential edits V's ratification triggers (flagged, not made)\n769\t\n770\tApplying §7.1 and §7.2 takes Home 2 from **22** to **24** condition marks. Three\n771\tartifacts assert that count and would need the mechanical follow-through:\n772\t\n773\t1. **`AC-65`** (Plan.md §1.6) — *\"5 abstention kinds + **22** condition marks + 5\n774\t   terminal routes\"* → **24**.\n775\t2. **`FX-LG-04`** (`06-test-strategy.md` §6.2) — the kernel membership-and-count\n776\t   gate asserts the same triple; it fails until updated. **This is the gate working\n777\t   as designed** — it is exactly the test that catches an unplaced state.\n778\t3. **`02-data-model.md` §13** — the closed-enum inventory row *\"condition marks |\n779\t   **22**, not restated here\"* → **24**.\n780\t\n781\t`04-api-contract.md` §10 imports the membership by citation and needs no edit.\n782\t\n783\t### 7.5 · Draft edit note for §12.3, in the pack's own convention\n784\t\n785\t```\n786\t**Edit note — <date>** `RULED(DR-084; DR-020 knob 7)` · **Rows 23\n787\t(`UNVERIFIED-CITATION`) and 24 (`CITATION-RECHECK-FAILED`) were\n788\tadded on this date.** They are the two members of the eight typed\n789\tcitation-failure routes that surface to a reader, placed here because S-13\n790\tmakes this table the only place a typed state may be minted. The routes\n791\tthemselves are a closed evidence-subsystem enum in `packages/kernel` and are\n792\tnot members of this table. Authority: DR-084 (architecture proposes, V\n793\tratifies) over DR-020 knob 7's \"eight typed failure routes from day one\";\n794\tproposal at `docs/missions/2026-08-06-v3-programming/ratification/\n795\tcitation-routes.md`. AC-65's count becomes 5 + 24 + 5.\n796\t```\n797\t\n798\t---\n799\t\n800\t## 8. The routes ship; the gate does not (DR-088 · AC-78 · P16)\n801\t\n802\t**DR-020 knob 7 has two clauses and they ship at different times.** The **eight\n803\ttyped failure routes** ship *\"from day one\"* — this document's subject. The\n804\t**hard-kill gate** *\"auto-activates when V3's character-level quote matcher ships\n805\tand validates\"* — and **DR-088** rules that auto-activation **counts as shipped\n806\tdark**: the gate is **written when the matcher validates, never shipped inert**,\n807\twith a **NOT-SHIPPED attestation** (`FX-DEF-01`, **P18**) standing in the\n808\tacceptance bundle until then.\n809\t\n810\t**What that forbids in this proposal, and what it permits.**\n811\t\n812\t- **Forbidden — and absent here:** any global kill predicate over the eight; any\n813\t  register key that would enable one; any column (`is_hard_killed`, `kill_reason`)\n814\t  whose only consumer is the unwritten gate. None appears in §4 or §6.\n815\t- **Permitted — and used here:** the consequences the pack **already rules** for\n816\t  each failure mode. R2's serve block is **Q51's**, ruled and always-on (S-10: the\n817\t  sole never-disabled serving invariant). R4's write refusal is **E-7's `CHECK`**,\n818\t  already accepted at DR-099 A-06. R5's refusal is **E-13's**. None of these is the\n819\t  citation hard-kill gate; each predates it and each fires today.\n820\t\n821\t**What the gate will consume when it is written.** Recording the routes now is\n822\tprecisely what makes the gate **writable later without shipping it now** — the\n823\tsame shape **P16** gives shadow mode. The kill set is **V's to name at that\n824\tsitting**; the seat notes only that **R8 `SPAN_MISMATCH`** and **R2\n825\t`CITATION_UNBACKED`** are its natural subjects, and **records no predicate**.\n826\t\n827\t---\n828\t\n829\t## 9. Scrutiny points — what a reviewer must check, and what V must rule\n830\t\n831\t| # | The point | The seat's position |\n832\t|---:|---|---|\n833\t| **SP-1** | **Should `VERIFIED` be a ninth member** instead of a separate `outcome` column? | **No.** E-8 says *failures* take the eight; a ninth member makes the enum nine and breaks DR-020 knob 7's count. **V's call.** |\n834\t| **SP-2** | **Is R1 `NO_SOURCE_FOUND` a citation failure at all**, or an abstention wearing a route? | It is **both-facing by design**: the attempt was made (query issued, retrieval ran, zero returned) and the honest nothing must be a **named** route, or it becomes the enum's blind spot. Dropping it re-opens residue at rung 1. |\n835\t| **SP-3** | **R2 merges two conditions** — a phantom source and a real source with no archived locator. Should they be two routes? | **No**, on the individuation rule §4 applies throughout: identical serve consequence (Q51 blocks) and identical loudness ⇒ one route, with the limb recorded. Splitting them costs a slot the eight cannot spare. |\n836\t| **SP-4** | **R6 merges five causes** *(rev 1: three · rev 2: four · rev 3: five)*, **three of which are engine-side** rather than source-side. Should an engine-side compare failure be its own route? | **Still open, and the pressure on it has only grown** — each review round widened the merge. The seat's position remains **no**: the serve consequence is identical (shown, not claimed verified), the cause is recorded, and the ledger entry is loud internally (S-19, digest-visible). **Grok pressed it and accepted** (the mark states the shared observable; withdrawing on an execution failure would over-claim). **Codex required it not be papered over** — so each round made the reason domain **provably total** rather than quietly widening one member. **Splitting makes the enum nine, which is DR-084's count to change. V's call, not the seat's** — rider **R6-a**. |\n837\t| **SP-5** | **A source-level citation with no span cited** lands in R6 and is therefore *not verified*. Is that too harsh? | It is **honest**: nothing was checked character-for-character. E-8 obliges span extraction, so this is the exception, not the norm — and only **two** routes block or refuse outright. **Grok pressed this and judged it honest**, naming the real risk as **badge dilution** — a product question for V at ratification, whose fix is *extract spans as the norm*, not drop the label so unchecked cites look checked. |\n838\t| **SP-6** | **Rung order is load-bearing** (currency before exactness). Is the order part of the ratification? | **Yes.** Without it, a source edited after retrieval is recorded as an engine misquote. The order should be ratified with the membership. **Rev 2 adds a second load-bearing ordering**: rung 1's rule that **`P_abs` is consulted only when `P_ref` is false** (§3.1), and rung 2's rule that the depth is **the attempt's, not the source's** (§3.2). Both are ratifiable properties, not implementation latitude. |\n839\t| **SP-7** | **The two new marks take Home 2 from 22 to 24**, breaking `FX-LG-04` and `AC-65` until the follow-through lands (§7.4). | Flagged, not applied. The break is the count gate **doing its job**. |\n840\t| **SP-8** | ~~Access depth has no row in `02-data-model.md` §13's closed-enum inventory.~~ **RESOLVED — withdrawn in rev 4.** | **The inventory limb is closed and this scrutiny point is retired.** `02-data-model.md` **§13 now carries an `access depth` row** — all three E-7 members, *\"three-valued and REQUIRED, not an adjective\"*, `kernel` home, `evidence.source_record` `CHECK` as its enforcement site — and **§11A.1 now names the `access_depth` column explicitly and points at §13**. Rev 1 raised this against the then-current text; a fold-in has since landed and the seat **re-read §13 and §11A.1 to confirm it** rather than carrying the claim forward. **What remains is a different limb, and it is not this one:** **CARRIER-1** (§9.1) is a **placement** gap — the record is held **per source** where E-7 obliges it **per opening** — and the §13 row's own enforcement site (`evidence.source_record`) is the evidence that the placement is per-source. **Only CARRIER-1 goes to the fold-in ticket.** |\n841\t| **SP-9** | The **`citation_route_record` field list (§6)** is a proposal against `02-data-model.md` §11A.1, which currently declares the table with no columns. | Fold-in belongs to a separate ticket **after** ratification; nothing was edited in `02-data-model.md`. |\n842\t| **SP-10** | **`DEFECT`'s definition may not cover the branch R2 lands on.** §12.3 mark **#14** says `DEFECT` means *\"composition could not be conformed in two attempts, or the verdict failed its stranger check\"* — **two causes**. But **S-7's state machine** gives the `DEFECT` badge to `COMPONENTS_ONLY` reached by *\"a blocking gate that prose cannot repair\"*, which includes a **Q51 provenance failure** (S-9 names that terminal's fixture explicitly). | Found in passing, **outside this file contract and outside DR-084's scope**. Raised, not resolved: it is either a harmless summary in #14's *Says* column or an uncovered branch, and only V can touch §12.3. **This proposal does not depend on the answer** — R2's serve consequence is S-7's, whatever #14's text is amended to say. |\n843\t| **SP-11** | **Do `ran` claims write citation routes at all?** The ladder is **harvest-document native** — its producers are Q16/Q40, its carrier is `source_ref → source_record`, and its founding home is E-8's *\"citation failures\"*. A probe-backed claim living only in `probe_capture` / `instrument_certification` has **no `source_record`** and would read as **R2** under a literal reading, which is harsh for a real measurement. | Raised by **Grok (SR-2)** and carried. Where a probe **is** projected into a `source_record`, a dead instrument locator reaches **R3** correctly. Where it is not, the claim already has **Q51's locator gate** and the probe tables, and writes no `citation_route_record` row (D3's boundary, §5.5). **The fold-in ticket must say which**, and the seat recommends stating it in `02-data-model.md` §11A.1 rather than widening the enum — **it is a documentation question, not a residue hole in E-8's domain**. |\n844\t\n845\t---\n846\t\n847\t### 9.1 Riders — behaviour V may want, and one carrier the data model owes\n848\t\n849\t*New in rev 2, and the direct product of Codex finding 4. Each rider is a\n850\tconsequence a reasonable reader might expect, which **no ruled requirement\n851\tsupplies**. They are written here so V can adopt them **explicitly**, and so that\n852\tnothing in §4 has to pretend the pack already says them.*\n853\t\n854\t| Rider | What it would add | Why it is not asserted | Nearest ruled analogue |\n855\t|---|---|---|---|\n856\t| **R5-a** | A consequence for **`VERSION_DRIFT`** beyond disclosure — a cap, a badge, or a refusal | **E-13's ruled trigger is newest-source age against `as_of`**, not per-source version identity. Extending E-13 to any edited source is an extension both reviewers named (Codex finding 4; Grok **SR-1**) | E-13's fast/slow branch; `STALE` (#10), which is **answer-scoped** and fires on DR-015's watched trigger |\n857\t| **R6-a** | **Splitting the two engine-side reasons — `COMPARE_EXECUTION_NOT_OK` and `COMPARE_RESULT_MISSING` — out of R6** into their own route, so an engine-side failure is never labelled with a source-side limit. Either reason alone, or both together | Moving **either or both** into one engine-side route makes the enum **nine**, and **DR-020 knob 7's count is V's**. The seat holds the merge (SP-4) and records the option rather than taking it | R6's reason field already separates the causes on the record |\n858\t| **R7/R8-a** | **Striking the failed span from the served text** and asserting **partial-answer survival** — *\"one quote lost, never the answer\"* | **S-9e rules eviction of an unreplayable component *number***; **Q40 rules a typed outcome**, not a text-surface remedy. Rev 1 asserted the extension; rev 2 does not | **S-9e** (§12.1c) and `MISSING-NUMBER` (#22) — the same shape, ruled for numbers |\n859\t\n860\t**None of the three is needed for closure.** The partition, the disjointness and the\n861\tloudness floor all hold without them; they change **what a reader sees after** a\n862\troute fires, never **which** route fires.\n863\t\n864\t**CARRIER-1 — a carrier, not a behaviour** *(new in rev 3; the one rider closure\n865\tdoes depend on)*\n866\t\n867\t| | |\n868\t|---|---|\n869\t| **What it adds** | `evidence.citation_route_record.attempt_access_depth` — E-7's **existing** three values, written by **each attempt that opens something**, plus `opening_action_ref` and the **four `CHECK`s** at §3.2. **Rev 4 scoping:** the value is **`NULL` exactly on the rung-1 terminals R1 and R2**, where no opening occurred and no honest E-7 value exists, and **required on every row that descends to rung 2** |\n870\t| **Why it is owed** | **E-7 obliges the depth as a *required record* of an opening**, and **Q40 reopens the locators** — a second opening. `02-data-model.md` §11A.1 places the record on **`source_record`**, which is **per source**, so **two openings of one source cannot disagree**. That is the mirror of §6.1's own `action_scope` reasoning (*\"a property of the **kind**, not of the row … two rows of one kind could disagree — a second authority for one fact\"*), applied in the opposite direction |\n871\t| **Why it is not a mint** | **No new vocabulary and no new obligation** — E-7's three values, E-7's requirement, and `02-data-model.md` **§13 already inventories the vocabulary** with its `kernel` home and its `evidence.source_record` enforcement site. What moves is **where one opening's value is written**: a **placement** repair, not a rule V must make |\n872\t| **Who applies it** | The **`02-data-model.md` §11A.1 fold-in ticket**, with SP-9. **Not this ticket** — §11A.1 is V-accepted text |\n873\t| **What depends on it** | **Rung 2's totality for `OK` reopen attempts.** The other two cases (non-`OK` outcome; harvest attempt) are settled by ruled fields today. Without CARRIER-1 a reopen that succeeds at a **changed** depth has no representable value — and because the write is **required for any attempt that opens** (§3.2 `CHECK` 1), that is a **write-time failure, never a silent inheritance of `OPENED_FULL`** |\n874\t\n875\t### 9.2 Rev 1 → rev 2 change log\n876\t\n877\t| Finding | Verdict | Repair |\n878\t|---|---|---|\n879\t| **Codex 1** — rung 1 non-total | **Valid.** Rev 1's closure table and its leaf triggers disagreed, and the leaf triggers left *incomplete record + covering absence row* homeless | Rung 1 restated over **`P_ref` / `P_rec` / `P_abs`** with a full truth table (**§3.1**); R1 and R2's triggers rewritten to match it; §5.1 rung-1 row rewritten |\n880\t| **Codex 2** — later access loss escapes the obliged fields | **Valid, and the sharpest of the four.** Rev 1 tested a **historical** source-scoped depth; Grok's ADV-4 assumed the reopen state was carried without asking which field carries it | Rung 2 made **attempt-scoped** and bound to the access-depth / recheck action **A-2 already obliges**, with any non-`OK` typed outcome ⇒ `ACCESS_BLOCKED` (**§3.2**). **No new column** (AC-85/AC-88) and **no new route**. The harvest-success / reopen-failure sequence is re-run in §3.2 and §5.1 |\n881\t| **Codex 3** — R6's reason enum incomplete | **Valid.** `BLOCKED`, `REFUSED` and *never-executed* were unrepresentable | Reason domain rebuilt as a **four-member ordered split** whose fourth member **references** the ledger outcome instead of restating it — total by construction and immune to ledger-vocabulary growth (**§4 · R6**). evidence.probe_capture(run_id, probe_capture_id),\n111\t  FOREIGN KEY (run_id, negative_probe_capture_ref) REFERENCES evidence.probe_capture(run_id, probe_capture_id)\n112\t);\n113\t\n114\tCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION evidence.validate_instrument_certification()\n115\tRETURNS trigger\n116\tLANGUAGE plpgsql\n117\tAS $$\n118\tDECLARE positive_row evidence.probe_capture%ROWTYPE;\n119\tDECLARE negative_row evidence.probe_capture%ROWTYPE;\n120\tDECLARE derived_outcome text;\n121\tBEGIN\n122\t  SELECT * INTO positive_row FROM evidence.probe_capture WHERE probe_capture_id = NEW.positive_probe_capture_ref;\n123\t  SELECT * INTO negative_row FROM evidence.probe_capture WHERE probe_capture_id = NEW.negative_probe_capture_ref;\n124\t  IF positive_row.run_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.run_id OR negative_row.run_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.run_id\n125\t    OR positive_row.instrument_ref IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.instrument_ref\n126\t    OR negative_row.instrument_ref IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.instrument_ref THEN\n127\t    RAISE EXCEPTION 'instrument certification receipt scope mismatch' USING ERRCODE = '23514';\n128\t  END IF;\n129\t  IF positive_row.expected_polarity <> 'POSITIVE' OR negative_row.expected_polarity <> 'NEGATIVE' THEN\n130\t    RAISE EXCEPTION 'instrument certification requires known-positive and known-negative receipts' USING ERRCODE = '23514';\n131\t  END IF;\n132\t  derived_outcome := CASE\n133\t    WHEN positive_row.observed_outcome = 'POSITIVE' AND negative_row.observed_outcome = 'NEGATIVE'\n134\t      THEN 'CERTIFIED'\n135\t    ELSE 'UNINSTRUMENTED'\n136\t  END;\n137\t  IF NEW.outcome <> derived_outcome THEN\n138\t    RAISE EXCEPTION 'instrument certification outcome must be derived from receipts' USING ERRCODE = '23514';\n139\t  END IF;\n140\t  RETURN NEW;\n141\tEND;\n142\t$$;\n143\t\n144\tDO $$\n145\tBEGIN\n146\t  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_trigger WHERE tgname = 'validate_instrument_certification') THEN\n147\t    CREATE TRIGGER validate_instrument_certification\n148\t    BEFORE INSERT ON evidence.instrument_certification\n149\t    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION evidence.validate_citation_route_record()\n208\tRETURNS trigger\n209\tLANGUAGE plpgsql\n210\tAS $$\n211\tDECLARE opening_outcome text;\n212\tDECLARE opening_run uuid;\n213\tDECLARE harvest_depth text;\n214\tBEGIN\n215\t  IF NEW.opening_action_ref IS NOT NULL THEN\n216\t    SELECT outcome, run_id INTO opening_outcome, opening_run\n217\t    FROM ledger.ledger_entry WHERE ledger_entry_id = NEW.opening_action_ref;\n218\t    IF opening_run IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.run_id THEN\n219\t      RAISE EXCEPTION 'citation opening action belongs to a different run' USING ERRCODE = '23514';\n220\t    END IF;\n221\t    IF opening_outcome = 'SKIPPED_BY_BUDGET' THEN\n222\t      RAISE EXCEPTION 'protected citation comparison cannot be skipped by budget' USING ERRCODE = '23514';\n223\t    END IF;\n224\t    IF opening_outcome <> 'OK' AND NEW.attempt_access_depth <> 'ACCESS_BLOCKED' THEN\n225\t      RAISE EXCEPTION 'non-OK opening requires ACCESS_BLOCKED' USING ERRCODE = '23514';\n226\t    END IF;\n227\t  END IF;\n228\t  IF NEW.row_id = 'Q16' AND NEW.source_ref IS NOT NULL THEN\n229\t    SELECT access_depth INTO harvest_depth FROM evidence.source_record WHERE source_record_id = NEW.source_ref;\n230\t    IF NEW.attempt_access_depth IS DISTINCT FROM harvest_depth THEN\n231\t      RAISE EXCEPTION 'Q16 attempt depth must equal the harvested source depth' USING ERRCODE = '23514';\n232\t    END IF;\n233\t  END IF;\n234\t  RETURN NEW;\n235\tEND;\n236\t$$;\n237\t\n238\tDO $$\n239\tBEGIN\n240\t  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_trigger WHERE tgname = 'validate_citation_route_record') THEN\n241\t    CREATE TRIGGER validate_citation_route_record\n242\t    BEFORE INSERT ON evidence.citation_route_record\n243\t    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION evidence.validate_citation_route_record();\n244\t  END IF;\n245\tEND;\n246\t$$;\n247\t\n248\tDO $$\n249\tDECLARE table_name text;\n250\tBEGIN\n251\t  FOREACH table_name IN ARRAY ARRAY[\n252\t    'query_set', 'query_amendment', 'source_record', 'evidence_item',\n253\t    'absence_row', 'probe_capture', 'instrument_certification', 'citation_route_record'\n254\t  ] LOOP\n255\t    IF NOT EXISTS (\n256\t      SELECT 1 FROM pg_trigger trigger_row\n257\t      JOIN pg_class table_row ON table_row.oid = trigger_row.tgrelid\n258\t      JOIN pg_namespace schema_row ON schema_row.oid = table_row.relnamespace\n259\t      WHERE trigger_row.tgname = 'reject_mutation'\n260\t        AND schema_row.nspname = 'evidence' AND table_row.relname = table_name\n261\t    ) THEN\n262\t      EXECUTE format(\n263\t        'CREATE TRIGGER reject_mutation BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON evidence.%I FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.reject_mutation()',\n264\t        table_name\n265\t      );\n266\t    END IF;\n267\t  END LOOP;\n268\tEND;\n269\t$$;\n270\t\n271\tCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS evidence_item_cluster_lookup\n272\t  ON evidence.evidence_item (run_id, provenance_cluster_key);\n273\tCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS citation_route_current_attempt\n274\t  ON evidence.citation_route_record (run_id, assertion_ref, at_seq DESC);\n275\t"}]},"uuid":"66b5a486-9031-40a1-ab52-3bb094536177","timestamp":"2026-08-08T15:25:08.915Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"285e9ffe-8596-4b59-ba64-0989ed4a94d6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/vladmihaimiron/Documents/DebateAI-V3","sessionId":"a8fcf2e3-bdd1-4a10-bcd6-3ec887012907","version":"2.1.221","gitBranch":"main","slug":"hidden-riding-bengio"}

polarity: QueryPolarity }>[];\n29\t  readonly contentHash: string;\n30\t} {\n31\t  if (seeds.some((seed) => !seed.derivedFromQuestion)) {\n32\t    throw new TypedDomainError(\"OFF_PLAN_QUERY_REFUSED\", \"Retrieval queries must be derived from the frozen question\");\n33\t  }\n34\t  const deduplicated = new Map<string, Readonly<{ text: string; polarity: QueryPolarity }>>();\n35\t  for (const seed of seeds) {\n36\t    const text = nonBlank(seed.text, \"BLANK_QUERY_REFUSED\");\n37\t    deduplicated.set(`${seed.polarity}\\0${text}`, Object.freeze({ text, polarity: seed.polarity }));\n38\t  }\n39\t  const queries = Object.freeze([...deduplicated.values()]);\n40\t  if (queries.length === 0 || !queries.some((query) => query.polarity === \"DISCONFIRMING\")) {\n41\t    throw new TypedDomainError(\"OPPOSITION_QUERY_REQUIRED\", \"A frozen query set must include disconfirming terms\");\n42\t  }\n43\t  const contentHash = createHash(\"sha256\").update(JSON.stringify(queries)).digest(\"hex\");\n44\t  return Object.freeze({ queries, contentHash });\n45\t}\n46\t\n47\texport type QueryAmendmentKind = \"MECHANICAL_REPAIR\" | \"SEMANTIC_REAIM\";\n48\texport function createQueryAmendment(input: {\n49\t  readonly querySetRef: string;\n50\t  readonly kind: QueryAmendmentKind;\n51\t  readonly amendedQuery: string;\n52\t  readonly reason: string;\n53\t}): Readonly<typeof input & { confirmationPower: \"FULL\" | \"EXPLORATION_ONLY\" }> {\n54\t  const result = {\n55\t    querySetRef: nonBlank(input.querySetRef, \"QUERY_SET_REF_REQUIRED\"),\n56\t    kind: input.kind,\n57\t    amendedQuery: nonBlank(input.amendedQuery, \"AMENDED_QUERY_REQUIRED\"),\n58\t    reason: nonBlank(input.reason, \"AMENDMENT_REASON_REQUIRED\"),\n59\t    confirmationPower: input.kind === \"MECHANICAL_REPAIR\" ? \"FULL\" as const : \"EXPLORATION_ONLY\" as const\n60\t  };\n61\t  return Object.freeze(result);\n62\t}\n63\t\n64\texport type SubjectRelevance = \"ON_SUBJECT\" | \"PARTLY_RELEVANT\" | \"OFF_SUBJECT\";\n65\texport function assessAdmissibility(input: { readonly relevance: SubjectRelevance; Object.freeze({ outcome: \"ADMITTED_DOWNGRADED\", scoreAllowed: true, visibleDowngrade: reason })\n74\t    : Object.freeze({ outcome: \"REJECTED\", scoreAllowed: false, visibleDowngrade: reason });\n75\t}\n76\t\n77\texport function deriveProvenanceClusterKey(input: {\n78\t  readonly studyOrDatasetIdentity: string | null;\n79\t  readonly sourceDomain: string | null;\n80\t  readonly publisher: string | null;\n81\t  readonly producingRunId: string;\n82\t  readonly modelFamily: string;\n83\t  readonly nodeId: string;\n84\t}): string {\n85\t  const identity = input.studyOrDatasetIdentity?.trim()\n86\t    || [input.sourceDomain?.trim(), input.publisher?.trim()].filter(Boolean).join(\"|\");\n87\t  if (!identity) return `singleton:${nonBlank(input.nodeId, \"NODE_ID_REQUIRED\")}`;\n88\t  return `${identity}|${nonBlank(input.producingRunId, \"PRODUCING_RUN_REQUIRED\")}|${nonBlank(input.modelFamily, \"MODEL_FAMILY_REQUIRED\")}`;\n89\t}\n90\t\n91\texport function evaluateFreshness(input: {\n92\t  readonly newestRetrievedAt: Date;\n93\t  readonly asOf: Date;\n94\t  readonly maxAgeMs: number;\n95\t  readonly registerRowRef: string;\n96\t  readonly pace: \"FAST_MOVING\" | \"SLOW_OR_STATIC\";\n97\t}): {\n98\t  readonly state: \"CURRENT\" | \"STALE\";\n99\t  readonly consequence: \"SERVE\" | \"REFUSE\" | \"SERVE_WITH_STALENESS\";\n100\t  readonly registerRowRef: string;\n101\t} {\n102\t  if (!Number.isFinite(input.maxAgeMs) || input.maxAgeMs < 0) throw new TypedDomainError(\"FRESHNESS_BOUND_INVALID\", \"Freshness requires a non-negative register bound\");\n103\t  const registerRowRef = nonBlank(input.registerRowRef, \"FRESHNESS_REGISTER_ROW_REQUIRED\");\n104\t  const stale = input.asOf.getTime() - input.newestRetrievedAt.getTime() > input.maxAgeMs;\n105\t  return Object.freeze({\n106\t    state: stale ? \"STALE\" : \"CURRENT\",\n107\t    consequence: stale ?
